# Blind FLoRA audit run log

- Launch: see run_manifest.json (UTC). Deadlines: coding +8h, adjudication +9.5h, checkpoint +10h.
- Read brief in full, repository instructions, shared preferences and box preferences. Confirmed branch audit/flora-blind-2026-10; working tree initially clean.
- Frozen input: 2,818 rows, 2,344 works, 2,295 single-row works, 11 projects with >8 rows. Immutable indices are zero based. All CSV values read as strings.
- Decisions: only identifier normalization treats literal NA as missing. Source texts are local active processing, gitignored; never committed. Coding tasks receive only sanitized replication metadata, codebook, schema and assigned paths. Infrastructure child is permanently ineligible for blind coding.
- Requested Codex model/options and Gemini 4 Argon (High) availability verified. Included subscription tools; no incremental paid API run authorized.
- Initial ramp limits count infrastructure child too, conservatively keeping total child concurrency at or below 10.
- 09:21 UTC: Adapter tests passed before first coding dispatch (replication, all frozen reproduction outcomes, missing DOI, multi-original). Dispatched first two blind 5-work batches; infrastructure occupies third initial slot. First dispatch ~1.5 minutes after recorded launch.
- Recovery schedule: scheduled-task:command:mcp:ec105078-9e31-4415-b39c-1709fd0d8b4b:schedule-task:flora-unattended-checkpoint-v1, every 5 minutes, first next run 09:26:20 UTC. Remove at completion or hard stop; never leave indefinite audit work scheduled.
- 09:25 UTC: 9 saved codings pass schema and SHA256 validation. Comparison engine's 25 tests pass; provisional results contain all 2,818 rows. Refilled first completed worker with batch 00002.
- First Argon triage: social-media willingness-to-pay temporal comparisons need substantive judgment about successful versus mixed; welfare-policy study reconstructs rather than simply reuses original data, leaving type/outcome non-obvious; autism abstract says methods were replicated/extended but does not plainly settle original-result agreement. All three works sent to Gemini 4 Argon (High), one call per work, source-quote validation required. No errors confirmed from blind disagreement alone.
- Active initial Argon runner retained as terminal session 68861; collect before another runner. Later runners use a nonblocking lock to keep global Argon concurrency <=4. Raw outputs and validated decisions remain under audit paths.
- Parent engine review: surname-first references with initials were not handled reliably by pilot helper; fixed citation parsing, including multipart family names, and added a targeted test. All 26 tests pass. Attempted coverage now includes dispatch logs; per-work times moved to work_times.csv to keep REPORT.md short.
- 09:29 UTC: First two Argon decisions validated against saved text. Autism abstract remains unverified; no unseen-full-text absence inferred. Heliyon outcome resolved as mixed (shipped successful); parent confirmed quoted passage in source and retained four consecutive supporting sentences. One outcome error confirmed so far; preliminary coverage is access-dependent and not representative.
- +10 min ramp: five coding workers active, including a dedicated large-project lane (25 shipped pairs; this count never enters the coder prompt). Text acquisition and enumeration remain blind.
- Task summaries can lag checkpoint artifacts: batch 00000 reported one no_text, but its assigned final file 4e3f516d5e45daab.json is a valid done coding with matching saved full-text SHA256. Only that batch was assigned this work. Coverage is calculated from validated files, not self-reported counts; no no_text record was observed by the parent for this key.
- Mid-run source-integrity check: output/flora.csv matches original SHA256. Academic style guide read; report keeps field-specific denominators, unresolved bounds and access-dependent coverage limits.
- 09:35 UTC: Initial three Argon calls collected; all validated on first attempt (95s,124s,483s). Welfare-policy record adjudicated reproduction rather than replication, with computational issues and robustness challenges. Parent accepted source-verified decisions. Engine now accepts the two-axis correction object after a validated type correction; targeted regression test added (27 tests pass). This confirms a second row with errors. No_text/uncertain cases remain unverified.
- Argon terminal session 68861 has finished; no Argon processes from that runner remain. Future runners use the global lock and select at most four work groups. First large project returned 25 independently enumerated targets; the lane was immediately assigned another project.
- Worker scratch metadata initially matched a broad JSON staging glob; removed those two scratch entries from Git immediately and tightened ignore/discovery rules. Future staging filters exact 16-hex coding filenames. Source texts were never staged.
- 09:37 UTC triage: Shifting attitudes (4e3f516d5e45daab), failed vs mixed, is OBVIOUS from Results/Table 3: authors explicitly retain the prior explicit preference while contrasting changed warmer feelings and implicit preference. Source quote verified; resolved mixed without Argon. Next non-obvious batch: 37f000537de70d42 (which cited genetic-association paper is actually retested), 7113daab150388e1 (mixed/failed and extra target scope), e340bb8dc4ca59b5 (successful/mixed overall-verdict scope), 4d22f5a1242960d7 (whether four additional targets are actual omitted retests). These require source interpretation rather than automatic matching.
- +20 min ramp reached eight coding workers. At 09:42 UTC,81 done codings with60 full texts and21 abstracts; seven no_text records are final. All88 exact-name records pass validation. The 165-target project is10.1098/rsos.231240 (140 shipped rows); many target outcomes are only structured subjective scores and lack complete-sentence quotes, so102 exact blind/shipped outcome agreements do not imply102 evaluable rows. These remain unverified pending source-grounded adjudication; standards are preserved.
- Comparison performance: repeatedly normalising the1.88MB large-project source made one comparison take minutes. Cached document normalisation; existing27 correctness tests pass and the complete comparison now takes2.25 seconds. No evidence-matching rule changed.
- Large adjudication transport decision: when inline shipped/blind payload exceeds90KB, save it as a scoped input JSON and pass its absolute path to Argon, avoiding the operating system's per-argument size limit. Argon receives the same complete content through an authorised file read; source text remains separate and gitignored.
- Adjudication validator correction: a missing_row verdict of shipped_right means no additional row is needed and therefore need not invent a replacement value. It still requires a source-verified quote, location and reason. The first genetics response exposed this case: its original-identity correction pairs the target to the shipped row, making the apparent missing target disappear. Added a regression test (28 tests pass). An unnecessary retry was already in flight under the original validator; preserve valid field decisions after collection rather than losing the whole work to that administrative shape issue.
